sin: torch.Tensor) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n        # Undo the HF permute\n        cos = cos[:, : cos.shape[1] // 2]\n        cos = torch.stack((cos, cos), dim=-1).flatten(-2)\n\n        sin = sin[:, : sin.shape[1] // 2]\n        sin = torch.stack((sin, sin), dim=-1).flatten(-2)\n\n        cos = cos.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)  # [1, 1, max_seq_len, dim]\n        sin = def __init__(\n        self,\n        dim: int,\n        max_position_embeddings: int,\n        base: float,\n        factor: float,\n        original_max_position_embeddings: int,\n        beta_fast: def yarn_find_correction_dim(num_rotations: float, dim: int, base: float, max_position_embeddings: int) -> float:\n        return (dim * math.log(max_position_embeddings / (num_rotations * 2 * math.pi))) / (2 * math.log(base))\n\n    # Find dim range bounds based on rotations\n    @staticmethod\n    def yarn_find_correction_range(\n        low_rot: float, high_rot: float, dim: int, base: float, max_position_embeddings: int\n    ) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n        low = math.floor(YarnRotaryEmbedding.yarn_find_correction_dim(low_rot, dim, base, max_position_embeddings))\n        high = math.ceil(YarnRotaryEmbedding.yarn_find_correction_dim(high_rot, dim, base, max_position_embeddings))\n        return max(low, 0), min(high, dim - 1)  # Clamp values just in case\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def yarn_get_mscale(scale: float, mscale: float) -> float:\n        if scale <= 1:\n            return 1.0\n        return 0.1 * mscale * math.log(scale) + 1.0\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def yarn_linear_ramp_mask(min: float, max: float, dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:\n        if min == max:\n            max += 0.001  # Prevent singularity\n\n        linear_func = (torch.arange(dim, dtype=torch.float32) - min) / (max - min)\n        ramp_func = torch.clamp(linear_func, 0, 1)\n        return ramp_func\n\n    def _set_cos_sin_cache(self, seq_len: int, device: Any, dtype: torch.dtype) -> None:\n        self.max_seq_len_cached = seq_len\n        dim = self.dim\n\n        freq_extra = 1.0 / (self.base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32, self.original_max_position_embeddings,\n        )\n        inv_freq_mask = 1.0 - YarnRotaryEmbedding.yarn_linear_ramp_mask(low, high, dim // 2).to(\n            device=device, dtype=torch.float32\n        )\n        inv_freq = freq_inter * (1 - inv_freq_mask) + freq_extra * inv_freq_mask\n        self.register_buffer(\"inv_freq\", inv_freq, persistent=False)\n\n        t = torch.arange(seq_len, emb.sin() * _mscale\n        cos, sin = self.permute_to_meta_format(cos, sin)\n\n        self.register_buffer(\"cos_cached\", cos.to(dtype), persistent=False)\n        self.register_buffer(\"sin_cached\", sin.to(dtype), persistent=False)\n\n\nclass LinearScaledRotaryEmbedding(ScaledRotaryEmbedding):\n    def __init__(\n        self, dim: int, Llama-3.x specific scaling\n        # Values obtained from grid search\n        low_freq_wavelen = self.orig_context_len / self.low_freq_factor\n        high_freq_wavelen = self.orig_context_len / self.high_freq_factor\n        new_freqs = []\n        for freq in freqs:\n            wavelen = 2 * math.pi / freq\n            if wavelen < high_freq_wavelen:\n                new_freqs.append(freq)\n            elif wavelen > low_freq_wavelen:\n                new_freqs.append(freq / self.scaling_factor)\n            else:\n                assert low_freq_wavelen != high_freq_wavelen\n                smooth = (self.orig_context_len / wavelen - self.low_freq_factor) / (\n                    self.high_freq_factor - self.low_freq_factor\n                )\n                new_freqs.append((1 - smooth) * freq / self.scaling_factor + smooth * freq)\n        return torch.tensor(new_freqs, dtype=freqs.dtype, device=freqs.device)\n\n\nclass Phi3RotaryEmbedding(ScaledRotaryEmbedding):\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        dim: int,\n        max_position_embeddings: int,\n        base: float,\n        original_max_position_embeddings: if (self.num_devices == 32 and batch_size > 1) else (None, None),\n                    mesh_shape=list(device.shape),\n                )\n                if self.is_mesh_device\n                else None\n            ),\n        )\n\n        # TODO: Colman, should this be TILE_SIZE or head_dim? Why should it be different for prefill and decode?\n        prefill_trans_mat_torch = get_rot_transformation_mat(dhead=head_dim)\n        self.transformation_mat_prefill = ttnn.from_torch(\n            prefill_trans_mat_torch,\n            device=device,\n            layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,\n            dtype=datatype,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n            mesh_mapper=replicate_tensor_to_mesh_mapper(device),\n        )\n\n    def get_both_trans_mats(self) -> Dict[str, ttnn.Tensor]:\n        assert self.transformation_mat is not None, \"Transformation matrix not initialized\"\n        assert self.transformation_mat_prefill is not None, \"Prefill Transformation matrix not initialized\"\n        return {\"decode\": self.transformation_mat, \"prefill\": self.transformation_mat_prefill}\n\n    def get_rot_idxs(self, position_idxs: torch.Tensor, on_host: bool = False) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n        assert isinstance(position_idxs, torch.Tensor), \"Position ids must be a torch tensor\"\n        assert len(position_idxs.shape) == 1, \"position idxs must be a [batch] tensor\"\n\n        batch = position_idxs.shape[0]\n        position_idxs = position_idxs.reshape(1, batch)  # [1, 1, 1, batch]\n        assert position_idxs.shape == (1, batch), \"position idxs must be a [1, batch] tensor\"\n        assert torch.min(position_idxs) >= 0, \"position idxs must be non-negative\"\n\n        # Add padding if needed\n        pad_size = nearest_32(batch) - batch\n        position_idxs = torch.nn.functional.pad(position_idxs, (0, pad_size), \"constant\", 0)\n\n        if on_host:  # If tensor is on host, don't pass a mesh mapper if single-device\n            rot_idxs = ttnn.as_tensor(\n                position_idxs,\n                dtype=ttnn.uint32,\n                layout=ttnn.ROW_MAJOR_LAYOUT,\n                mesh_mapper=replicate_tensor_to_mesh_mapper(self.device),\n            )\n        else:  # On device\n            rot_idxs = ttnn.as_tensor(\n                position_idxs,\n                dtype=ttnn.uint32,\n                layout=ttnn.ROW_MAJOR_LAYOUT,\n                device=self.device,\n                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n                mesh_mapper=replicate_tensor_to_mesh_mapper(self.device),\n            )\n\n        return rot_idxs\n\n    def get_rot_mats(\n        self, position_idxs: Union[torch.Tensor, ttnn.Tensor], return_rot_idxs: bool = False\n    ) -> List[ttnn.Tensor]:\n        device = self.device\n\n        # If position_idxs is a torch tensor, get the TTNN version of it\n        if isinstance(position_idxs, torch.Tensor):\n            rot_idxs = self.get_rot_idxs(position_idxs)\n        else:\n            rot_idxs = position_idxs\n            assert len(rot_idxs.shape) == 2 and rot_idxs.shape[0] == 1, \"rot_idxs must be a [1, batch] tensor\"\n        # Send the idxs to device\n\n        if rot_idxs.device != device:\n            rot_idxs = ttnn.to_device(rot_idxs, device, memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG)\n\n        embedding_layout = ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT\n        cos = ttnn.embedding(rot_idxs, self.cos_matrix, layout=embedding_layout)  # [1, batch, head_dim]\n        sin = ttnn.embedding(rot_idxs, self.sin_matrix, layout=embedding_layout)  # [1, batch, head_dim]\n\n        cos = ttnn.unsqueeze_to_4D(cos)  # [1, 1, batch, head_dim]\n        sin = ttnn.unsqueeze_to_4D(sin)  # [1, 1, batch, head_dim]\n\n        cos = ttnn.transpose(cos, 1, 2)  # [1, batch, 1[32], head_dim]\n        sin = ttnn.transpose(sin, 1, 2)  # [1, batch, 1[32], head_dim]\n\n        if self.batch_size_per_device_group % ttnn.TILE_SIZE != 0:\n            cos = cos[:, : self.batch_size_per_device_group, :, :]\n            sin = sin[:, : self.batch_size_per_device_group, :, :]\n\n        mem_config = ttnn.create_sharded_memory_config(\n            shape=(ttnn.TILE_SIZE, self.head_dim),\n            core_grid=self.batch_grid,\n            strategy=ttnn.ShardStrategy.HEIGHT,\n            orientation=ttnn.ShardOrientation.ROW_MAJOR,\n            use_height_and_width_as_shard_shape=True,\n        )\n\n        cos = ttnn.interleaved_to_sharded(cos, mem_config)  # [1, 1 (= batch / shard_num_cores), 1[32], self.head_dim]\n        sin = ttnn.interleaved_to_sharded(sin, mem_config)  # [1, 1 (= batch / shard_num_cores), 1[32], self.head_dim]\n\n        if return_rot_idxs:\n            return [cos, sin], rot_idxs\n        return [cos, sin]\n","numLines":539,"startLine":1,"totalLines":539}}}

cache_file_name=cache_name(\"wqkv_sharded_2d\"),\n   244→        )\n   245→\n   246→        def norm_reshard(x, norm, mode):\n   247→            \"\"\"Hack until RMSNorm supports height-sharded output config\"\"\"\n   248→            if mode == \"decode\":\n   249→                mem_cfg = x.memory_config()\n   250→                x = ttnn.to_memory_config(x, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG, dtype=x.dtype)\n   251→            x = norm(x, mode)\n   252→            if mode == \"decode\":\n   253→                x = ttnn.to_memory_config(x, mem_cfg, dtype=x.dtype)\n   254→            return x\n   255→\n   256→        if f\"{q_norm_str}.weight\" in state_dict:\n   257→            fn_q_norm = RMSNorm(\n   258→                device=self.mesh_device,\n   259→                dim=self.head_dim,\n   260→                eps=configuration.norm_eps,\n   261→                state_dict=state_dict,\n   262→                state_dict_prefix=None,  # we already prefix q_norm_str\n   263→                weight_cache_path=None if configuration.dummy_weights else weight_cache_path,\n   264→                weight_dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n   265→                weight_key=q_norm_str,\n   266→                add_unit_offset=self.rms_norm_add_unit_offset,\n   267→                is_distributed=False,\n   268→                sharded_program_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"SHARDED_NORM_ATTN_PRGM_CFG\"],\n   269→                sharded_output_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"]\n   270→                tt_ccl=self.tt_ccl,\n   271→            )\n   272→            self.q_norm = lambda x, mode: norm_reshard(x, fn_q_norm, mode)\n   273→        else:\n   274→            self.q_norm = lambda x, mode: x\n   275→\n   276→        if f\"{k_norm_str}.weight\" in state_dict:\n   277→            fn_k_norm = RMSNorm(\n   278→                device=self.mesh_device,\n   279→                dim=self.head_dim,\n   280→                eps=configuration.norm_eps,\n   281→                state_dict=state_dict,\n   282→                state_dict_prefix=None,  # we already prefix k_norm_str\n   283→                weight_cache_path=None if configuration.dummy_weights else weight_cache_path,\n   284→                weight_dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n   285→                weight_key=k_norm_str,\n   286→                add_unit_offset=self.rms_norm_add_unit_offset,\n   287→                is_distributed=False,\n   288→                sharded_program_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"SHARDED_NORM_ATTN_PRGM_CFG\"],\n   289→                sharded_output_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"],\n   290→                tt_ccl=self.tt_ccl,\n   291→            )\n   292→            self.k_norm = lambda x, mode: norm_reshard(x, fn_k_norm, mode)\n   293→        else:\n   294→            self.k_norm = lambda x, mode: x\n   295→\n   296→        # For ring topology we can use all gather matmul for wo\n   297→        self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul = self.model_config[\"USE_FUSED_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL\"]\n   298→        pt_wo = state_dict[f\"{wo_str}.weight\"].transpose(-1, -2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)\n   299→\n   300→        wo_mem_config = configuration.create_dram_sharded_mem_config(\n   301→            (configuration.n_heads * configuration.head_dim) // configuration.num_devices, configuration.dim\n   302→        )\n   303→\n   304→        self.wo = ttnn.as_tensor(\n   305→            pt_wo,\n   306→            dtype=self.wo_dtype,\n   307→            layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,\n   308→            device=self.mesh_device,\n   309→            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else wo_mem_config,\n   310→            mesh_mapper=ttnn.ShardTensor2dMesh(\n   311→                self.mesh_device,\n   312→                dims=(2, 3) if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else (3, 2),\n   313→                mesh_shape=configuration.cluster_shape,\n   314→            ),\n   315→            cache_file_name=(\n   316→                cache_name(\"wo_width_sharded_2d\") if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else cache_name(\"wo\")\n   317→            ),\n   318→        )\n   319→        if not use_paged_kv_cache:\n   320→            # vLLM provides its own kv cache\n   321→            self.init_kv_cache(configuration, weight_cache_path)\n   322→\n   323→        if configuration.query_pre_attn_scalar is not None:\n   324→            self.scale = configuration.query_pre_attn_scalar**-0.5\n   325→        else:\n   326→            self.scale = self.head_dim**-0.5\n   327→\n   328→    def init_kv_cache(self, configuration, weight_cache_path):\n   329→        \"\"\"\n   330→        Generates empty KV cache and pushed to device memory\n   331→        \"\"\"\n   332→\n   333→        if self.paged_attention_config:\n   334→            cache_k = torch.zeros(\n   335→                (\n   336→                    self.paged_attention_config.max_num_blocks,\n   337→                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n   338→                    self.paged_attention_config.block_size,\n   339→                    self.head_dim,\n   340→                )\n   341→            )\n   342→            cache_v = torch.zeros(\n   343→                (\n   344→                    self.paged_attention_config.max_num_blocks,\n   345→                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n   346→                    self.paged_attention_config.block_size,\n   347→                    self.head_dim,\n   348→                )\n   349→            )\n   350→        else:\n   351→            cache_k = torch.zeros(\n   352→                (\n   353→                    self.batch_size_per_device_group,\n   354→                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n   355→                    self.max_seq_len,\n   356→                    self.head_dim,\n   357→                )\n   358→            )\n   359→            cache_v = torch.zeros(\n   360→                (\n   361→                    self.batch_size_per_device_group,\n   362→                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n   363→                    self.max_seq_len,\n   364→                    self.head_dim,\n   365→                )\n   366→            )\n   367→\n   368→        self.layer_past = [\n   369→            ttnn.as_tensor(\n   370→                k_or_v,\n   371→                dtype=self.kv_cache_dtype,\n   372→                layout=self.model_config[\"ATTN_W_LAYOUT_TILE\"],\n   373→                device=self.mesh_device,\n   374→                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   375→                mesh_mapper=ttnn.ReplicateTensorToMesh(self.mesh_device),\n   376→                cache_file_name=(\n   377→                    f\"{weight_cache_path}/kvcache_{k_or_v.shape}\"\n   378→                    if weight_cache_path and not configuration.dummy_weights\n   379→                    else None\n   380→                ),\n   381→            )\n   382→            for k_or_v in [cache_k, cache_v]\n   383→        ]\n   384→\n   385→    def forward_decode(self, x: ttnn.Tensor, current_pos, rot_mats=None, page_table=None, kv_cache=None) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n   386→        \"\"\"\n   387→        x: (seq_len, 1, batch, dim)\n   388→        current_pos: (batch_size), current token position in the sequence for each user\n   389→        \"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→        ###\n   392→        # QKV matmuls\n   393→        # Use HiFi2 for DRAM-sharded matmuls as they are otherwise flop-bound. Loses 1 bit of activation precision.\n   394→        ###\n   395→\n   396→        xqkv_fused_sharded = ttnn.linear(\n   397→            x,\n   398→            self.wqkv,\n   399→            # bias=self.wqkv_bias,\n   400→            memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   401→            program_config=self.model_config[\"XQKV_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n   402→            compute_kernel_config=self.li_qkv_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   403→            dtype=self.ccl_dtype if self.TG else self.activation_dtype or ttnn.bfloat16,\n   404→        )\n   405→        # FIXME: File bug against dram-sharded matmuls with bias\n   406→        if self.wqkv_bias_decode:\n   407→            # select the bias tensor based on the number of tiles in the rows\n   408→            # WARNING: must not change the batch size between compiling and executing a trace\n   409→            num_tiles = int(math.ceil(xqkv_fused_sharded.shape[-2] / self.tile_size))\n   410→            xqkv_fused_sharded = xqkv_fused_sharded + self.wqkv_bias_decode[num_tiles - 1]\n   411→\n   412→        ttnn.deallocate(x)\n   413→        xqkv_fused = tt_all_reduce(\n   414→            xqkv_fused_sharded,\n   415→            self.mesh_device,\n   416→            self.tt_ccl,\n   417→            cluster_axis=1,\n   418→            num_reduce_scatter_links=self.num_reduce_scatter_links,\n   419→            num_all_gather_links=self.num_all_gather_links,\n   420→            memory_config=self.model_config[\"QKV_OUT_GATHERED_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[1]),\n   421→            sharded=True,\n   422→            dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n   423→            topology=self.ccl_topology,\n   424→        )\n   425→\n   426→        if self.TG:\n   427→            # TODO: Slice the fused_query_key_value tensor get batch=8\n   428→            xqkv_fused = ttnn.matmul(\n   429→                self.slice_mat,\n   430→                xqkv_fused,\n   431→                dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n   432→                memory_config=self.model_config[\"CREATE_HEAD_INPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n   433→            )\n   434→        else:\n   435→            # bfloat16 is required by nlp_create_qkv_heads_decode\n   436→            xqkv_fused = ttnn.sharded_to_interleaved(xqkv_fused_sharded, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG, ttnn.bfloat16)\n   437→\n   438→        ttnn.deallocate(xqkv_fused_sharded)\n   439→\n   440→        # Reshape such that true unpadded batch is tracked in shape\n   441→        fqkv_shape = xqkv_fused.shape\n   442→        xqkv_fused = ttnn.reshape(\n   443→            xqkv_fused, (1, 1, self.batch_size_per_device_group, fqkv_shape[3]), (1, 1, 32, fqkv_shape[3])\n   444→        )\n   445→\n   446→        ###\n   447→        # Reshape and rotary embeddings\n   448→        ###\n   449→        (\n   450→            q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD,\n   451→            k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD,\n   452→            v_heads_1BKD,\n   453→        ) = ttnn.experimental.nlp_create_qkv_heads_decode(\n   454→            xqkv_fused,\n   455→            num_heads=self.n_local_heads,\n   456→            num_kv_heads=self.n_local_kv_heads,\n   457→            memory_config=self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"],\n   458→        )\n   459→\n   460→        q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD = self.q_norm(q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD, mode=\"decode\")\n   461→        k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD = self.k_norm(k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD, mode=\"decode\")\n   462→\n   463→        ttnn.deallocate(xqkv_fused)\n   464→\n   465→        # Q Rotary Embeddings\n   466→        q_heads_1BQD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n   467→            q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD, rot_mats[0], rot_mats[1], self.transformation_mats[\"decode\"], is_decode_mode=True\n   468→        )\n   469→\n   470→        # K Rotary Embeddings\n   471→        k_heads_1BKD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n   472→            k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD, rot_mats[0], rot_mats[1], self.transformation_mats[\"decode\"], is_decode_mode=True\n   473→        )\n   474→\n   475→        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD)\n   476→        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD)\n   477→\n   478→        ###\n   479→        # KV update\n   480→        ###\n   481→        if kv_cache:\n   482→            keys = kv_cache[0]\n   483→            values = kv_cache[1]\n   484→        else:\n   485→            keys = self.layer_past[0]\n   486→            values = self.layer_past[1]\n   487→        # k_heads, [seqlen, n_kv_heads, bsz, head_dim]\n   488→        # v_heads [seqlen, n_kv_heads, bsz, head_dim]\n   489→        # keys, [max_batch_size, n_kv_heads // configuration.num_devices, max_seq_len, head_dim]\n   490→        ttnn.experimental.paged_update_cache(keys, k_heads_1BKD, update_idxs_tensor=current_pos, page_table=page_table)\n   491→        ttnn.experimental.paged_update_cache(\n   492→            values, v_heads_1BKD, update_idxs_tensor=current_pos, page_table=page_table\n   493→        )\n   494→\n   495→        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_1BKD)\n   496→        ttnn.deallocate(v_heads_1BKD)\n   497→\n   498→        # NOTE: Varying the batch size will result in slightly different outputs.\n   499→        # For example, a prompt w/ 1 user vs, the same prompt repeated N times for N users, will produce different outputs\n   500→        # This is because the SDPA op in decode mode has different number of reductions depending on batch size\n   501→        # Which leads to slightly different outputs from attention (due to accumulated errors)\n   502→        if page_table:\n   503→            attn_output_1G4D = ttnn.transformer.paged_scaled_dot_product_attention_decode(\n   504→                q_heads_1BQD,\n   505→                keys,\n   506→                values,\n   507→                cur_pos_tensor=current_pos,\n   508→                page_table_tensor=page_table,\n   509→                scale=self.scale,\n   510→                sliding_window_size=self.sliding_window,\n   511→                program_config=self.model_config[\"SDPA_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n   512→                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   513→                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   514→            )\n   515→        else:\n   516→            attn_output_1G4D = ttnn.transformer.scaled_dot_product_attention_decode(\n   517→                q_heads_1BQD,\n   518→                keys,\n   519→                values,\n   520→                cur_pos_tensor=current_pos,\n   521→                scale=self.scale,\n   522→                sliding_window_size=self.sliding_window,\n   523→                program_config=self.model_config[\"SDPA_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n   524→                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   525→                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,  # FIXME: why not L1 height sharded e.g. SCORES_BATCHED_MM_OUTPUT_MEMCFG?\n   526→            )\n   527→\n   528→        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_1BQD)\n   529→\n   530→        attn_output_11BH = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n   531→            attn_output_1G4D,\n   532→            memory_config=self.model_config[\"SCORES_BATCHED_MM_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"](self.batch_size_per_device_group),\n   533→        )\n   534→        attn_output_cat = ttnn.experimental.nlp_concat_heads_decode(\n   535→            attn_output_11BH,\n   536→            num_heads=self.n_local_heads,\n   537→        )\n   538→        ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_11BH)\n   539→        ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_1G4D)\n   540→\n   541→        if self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul:\n   542→            attn_output_cat = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n   543→                attn_output_cat, self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"]\n   544→            )\n   545→\n   546→            # Fused AGMM only valid for ring topology\n   547→            if self.ccl_topology == ttnn.Topology.Ring:\n   548→                _, dense_out_sharded = ttnn.experimental.all_gather_matmul_async(\n   549→                    attn_output_cat,\n   550→                    self.wo,\n   551→                    persistent_output_buffer=None,\n   552→                    dim=3,\n   553→                    multi_device_global_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_ag_semaphore_handles(),\n   554→                    all_gather_core_grid_offset=(0, 4),\n   555→                    barrier_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_barrier_semaphore_handle(),\n   556→                    num_links=1,\n   557→                    memory_config_ag=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n   558→                    memory_config_mm=self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"],\n   559→                    program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_PROGCFG\"],\n   560→                    compute_kernel_config=self.compute_kernel_config_hifi2,\n   561→                    chunks_per_sync=10,\n   562→                    num_workers_per_link=2,\n   563→                    num_buffers_per_channel=2,\n   564→                )\n   565→            else:\n   566→                all_gather_output = ttnn.experimental.all_gather_async(\n   567→                    attn_output_cat,\n   568→                    persistent_output_buffer=None,\n   569→                    dim=3,\n   570→                    multi_device_global_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_ag_semaphore_handles(),\n   571→                    num_links=1,\n   572→                    topology=self.ccl_topology,\n   573→                    memory_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n   574→                    barrier_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_barrier_semaphore_handle(),\n   575→                    chunks_per_sync=10,\n   576→                    num_workers_per_link=2,\n   577→                    num_buffers_per_channel=2,\n   578→                )\n   579→\n   580→                dense_out_sharded = ttnn.linear(\n   581→                    all_gather_output,\n   582→                    self.wo,\n   583→                    memory_config=self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"],\n   584→                    program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_PROGCFG\"],\n   585→                    compute_kernel_config=self.li_o_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   586→                )\n   587→\n   588→                ttnn.deallocate(all_gather_output)\n   589→            ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_cat)\n   590→            dense_out_sharded = ttnn.to_memory_config(dense_out_sharded, self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"])\n   591→            return dense_out_sharded\n   592→\n   593→        else:\n   594→            attn_output = tt_all_gather(\n   595→                attn_output_cat,\n   596→                self.mesh_device,\n   597→                self.tt_ccl,\n   598→                dim=2,\n   599→                cluster_axis=1,\n   600→                num_links=2,\n   601→                memory_config=self.model_config[\"GATHER_USERS_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[1]),\n   602→                sharded=True,\n   603→                # dtype=self.ccl_dtype,  # Running bf16 until we have SDPA output bfp8 df; otherwise we have two sharded to interleaved/interleaved to sharded conversions\n   604→            )\n   605→            if self.TG:\n   606→                attn_output = ttnn.to_memory_config(attn_output, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG)\n   607→                # user_selection_matrix = [1, 1, 32, 128]\n   608→                # user_selection_matrix @ activation -> [1, 1, 32, 128] * [1, 1, 128, 2048] -> [1, 1, 32, 2048]\n   609→                attn_output = ttnn.matmul(\n   610→                    self.user_selection_matrix,\n   611→                    attn_output,\n   612→                    core_grid=ttnn.CoreGrid(y=4, x=8),\n   613→                    dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n   614→                    memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   615→                )\n   616→\n   617→            # TODO: Fix this once self.TG supports dram-sharded matmuls\n   618→            dense_out_sharded = ttnn.matmul(\n   619→                attn_output,\n   620→                self.wo,\n   621→                core_grid=ttnn.CoreGrid(y=4, x=8) if self.TG else None,\n   622→                program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_OUTPUT_PROGCFG\"] if not self.TG else None,\n   623→                memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   624→                dtype=ttnn.bfloat8_b if self.TG else None,\n   625→                compute_kernel_config=self.li_o_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   626→            )\n   627→\n   628→            ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_cat)\n   629→\n   630→            # All reduce\n   631→            dense_out_reduced = tt_all_reduce(\n   632→                dense_out_sharded,\n   633→                self.mesh_device,\n   634→                self.tt_ccl,\n   635→                cluster_axis=0,\n   636→                num_reduce_scatter_links=self.num_reduce_scatter_links,\n   637→                num_all_gather_links=self.num_all_gather_links,\n   638→                dim=0 if (self.TG and self.hidden_size < 8192) else 3,\n   639→                topology=self.ccl_topology,\n   640→                memory_config=(\n   641→                    (\n   642→                        self.model_config[\"SELF_OUT_REDUCE_SCATTER_MEMCFG\"]\n   643→                        if self.hidden_size == 8192\n   644→                        else self.model_config[\"SELF_OUT_GATHERED_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[0])\n   645→                    )\n   646→                    if self.TG\n   647→                    else self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"]\n   648→                ),\n   649→                sharded=True,\n   650→                dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n   651→                use_composite=True if self.hidden_size == 8192 else False,\n   652→            )\n   653→\n   654→            if not self.TG:\n   655→                dense_out_reduced = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n   656→                    dense_out_reduced, self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"]\n   657→                )\n   658→\n   659→            return dense_out_reduced\n   660→\n   661→    def forward_prefill(\n   662→        self,\n   663→        x_11SH,\n   664→        rot_mats,\n   665→        user_id: int = 0,\n   666→        page_table=None,\n   667→        chunk_page_table=None,\n   668→        chunk_start_idx=None,\n   669→        kv_cache=None,\n   670→    ):\n   671→        seq_len = x_11SH.shape[-2]\n   672→        assert seq_len % 128 == 0 and seq_len > 0, \"Seqlen must be divisible by 128\"\n   673→        ###\n   674→        # QKV matmuls\n   675→        ###\n   676→\n   677→        # reshaping long sequence to matmul fit on device\n   678→        if seq_len > self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN:\n   679→            if seq_len % self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN != 0:\n   680→                raise ValueError(f\"seq_len {seq_len} must be divisible by {self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN}\")\n   681→            x_11SH = ttnn.reshape(x_11SH, [1, seq_len // self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN, self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN, -1])\n   682→\n   683→        xqkv_fused = ttnn.linear(\n   684→            x_11SH,\n   685→            self.wqkv,\n   686→            dtype=self.ccl_dtype if self.TG else self.activation_dtype or ttnn.bfloat16,\n   687→            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n   688→            compute_kernel_config=self.li_qkv_prefill_compute_kernel_cfg,\n   689→            program_config=self.model_config[\"XQKV_PREFILL_PROGCFG\"](seq_len),\n   690→        )\n   691→\n   692→        # FIXME: surely ttnn.linear bias should work?\n   693→        if self.wqkv_bias_prefill        wv_selected = torch.chunk(state_dict[f\"{wv_str}.weight\"], self.num_devices_per_group, dim=0)[i]\n\n            # Transpose the selected chunks\n            wq = torch.transpose(wq_selected, -2, -1)\n            wk = torch.transpose(wk_selected, -2, -1)\n            wv = torch.transpose(wv_selected, -2, -1)\n\n            qkv = torch.cat([wq, wk, wv], dim=-1)\n            qkv_list.append(qkv)\n\n        qkv_cat = torch.cat(qkv_list, dim=-1).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)\n\n        self.wqkv = ttnn.as_tensor(\n            qkv_cat,\n            dtype=self.wqkv_dtype,\n            layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,\n            device=self.mesh_device,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG if self.TG else wqkv_mem_config,\n            mesh_mapper=ttnn.ShardTensor2dMesh(\n                self.mesh_device, dims=(3, 2) if self.TG else (2, 3), mesh_shape=configuration.cluster_shape\n            ),\n            cache_file_name=cache_name(\"wqkv_sharded_2d\"),\n        )\n\n        def norm_reshard(x, norm, mode):\n            \"\"\"Hack until RMSNorm supports height-sharded output config\"\"\"\n            if mode == \"decode\":\n                mem_cfg = x.memory_config()\n                x = ttnn.to_memory_config(x, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG, dtype=x.dtype)\n            x = norm(x, mode)\n            if mode == \"decode\":\n                x = ttnn.to_memory_config(x, mem_cfg, dtype=x.dtype)\n            return x\n\n        if f\"{q_norm_str}.weight\" in state_dict:\n            fn_q_norm = RMSNorm(\n                device=self.mesh_device,\n                dim=self.head_dim,\n                eps=configuration.norm_eps,\n                state_dict=state_dict,\n                state_dict_prefix=None,  # we already prefix q_norm_str\n                weight_cache_path=None if configuration.dummy_weights else weight_cache_path,\n                weight_dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n                weight_key=q_norm_str,\n                add_unit_offset=self.rms_norm_add_unit_offset,\n                is_distributed=False,\n                sharded_program_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"SHARDED_NORM_ATTN_PRGM_CFG\"],\n                sharded_output_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"]\n                tt_ccl=self.tt_ccl,\n            )\n            self.q_norm = lambda x, mode: norm_reshard(x, fn_q_norm, mode)\n        else:\n            self.q_norm = lambda x, mode: x\n\n        if f\"{k_norm_str}.weight\" in state_dict:\n            fn_k_norm = RMSNorm(\n                device=self.mesh_device,\n                dim=self.head_dim,\n                eps=configuration.norm_eps,\n                state_dict=state_dict,\n                state_dict_prefix=None,  # we already prefix k_norm_str\n                weight_cache_path=None if configuration.dummy_weights else weight_cache_path,\n                weight_dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n                weight_key=k_norm_str,\n                add_unit_offset=self.rms_norm_add_unit_offset,\n                is_distributed=False,\n                sharded_program_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"SHARDED_NORM_ATTN_PRGM_CFG\"],\n                sharded_output_config=None,  # FIXME: add height-sharded support. self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"],\n                tt_ccl=self.tt_ccl,\n            )\n            self.k_norm = lambda x, mode: norm_reshard(x, fn_k_norm, mode)\n        else:\n            self.k_norm = lambda x, mode: x\n\n        # For ring topology we can use all gather matmul for wo\n        self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul = self.model_config[\"USE_FUSED_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL\"]\n        pt_wo = state_dict[f\"{wo_str}.weight\"].transpose(-1, -2).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)\n\n        wo_mem_config = configuration.create_dram_sharded_mem_config(\n            (configuration.n_heads * configuration.head_dim) // configuration.num_devices, configuration.dim\n        )\n\n        self.wo = ttnn.as_tensor(\n            pt_wo,\n            dtype=self.wo_dtype,\n            layout=ttnn.TILE_LAYOUT,\n            device=self.mesh_device,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else wo_mem_config,\n            mesh_mapper=ttnn.ShardTensor2dMesh(\n                self.mesh_device,\n                dims=(2, 3) if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else (3, 2),\n                mesh_shape=configuration.cluster_shape,\n            ),\n            cache_file_name=(\n                cache_name(\"wo_width_sharded_2d\") if (self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul or self.TG) else cache_name(\"wo\")\n            ),\n        )\n        if not use_paged_kv_cache:\n            # vLLM provides its own kv cache\n            self.init_kv_cache(configuration, weight_cache_path)\n\n        if configuration.query_pre_attn_scalar is not None:\n            self.scale = configuration.query_pre_attn_scalar**-0.5\n        else:\n            self.scale = self.head_dim**-0.5\n\n    def init_kv_cache(self, configuration, weight_cache_path):\n        \"\"\"\n        Generates empty KV cache and pushed to device memory\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self.paged_attention_config:\n            cache_k = torch.zeros(\n                (\n                    self.paged_attention_config.max_num_blocks,\n                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n                    self.paged_attention_config.block_size,\n                    self.head_dim,\n                )\n            )\n            cache_v = torch.zeros(\n                (\n                    self.paged_attention_config.max_num_blocks,\n                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n                    self.paged_attention_config.block_size,\n                    self.head_dim,\n                )\n            )\n        else:\n            cache_k = torch.zeros(\n                (\n                    self.batch_size_per_device_group,\n                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n                    self.max_seq_len,\n                    self.head_dim,\n                )\n            )\n            cache_v = torch.zeros(\n                (\n                    self.batch_size_per_device_group,\n                    self.n_local_kv_heads,\n                    self.max_seq_len,\n                    self.head_dim,\n                )\n            )\n\n        self.layer_past = [\n            ttnn.as_tensor(\n                k_or_v,\n                dtype=self.kv_cache_dtype,\n                layout=self.model_config[\"ATTN_W_LAYOUT_TILE\"],\n                device=self.mesh_device,\n                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n                mesh_mapper=ttnn.ReplicateTensorToMesh(self.mesh_device),\n                cache_file_name=(\n                    f\"{weight_cache_path}/kvcache_{k_or_v.shape}\"\n                    if weight_cache_path and not configuration.dummy_weights\n                    else None\n                ),\n            )\n            for k_or_v in [cache_k, cache_v]\n        ]\n\n    def forward_decode(self, x: ttnn.Tensor, current_pos, rot_mats=None, page_table=None, kv_cache=None) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n        \"\"\"\n        x: (seq_len, 1, batch, dim)\n        current_pos: (batch_size), current token position in the sequence for each user\n        \"\"\"\n\n        ###\n        # QKV matmuls\n        # Use HiFi2 for DRAM-sharded matmuls as they are otherwise flop-bound. Loses 1 bit of activation precision.\n        ###\n\n        xqkv_fused_sharded = ttnn.linear(\n            x,\n            self.wqkv,\n            # bias=self.wqkv_bias,\n            memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n            program_config=self.model_config[\"XQKV_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n            compute_kernel_config=self.li_qkv_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n            dtype=self.ccl_dtype if self.TG else self.activation_dtype or ttnn.bfloat16,\n        )\n        # FIXME: File bug against dram-sharded matmuls with bias\n        if self.wqkv_bias_decode:\n            # select the bias tensor based on the number of tiles in the rows\n            # WARNING: must not change the batch size between compiling and executing a trace\n            num_tiles = int(math.ceil(xqkv_fused_sharded.shape[-2] / self.tile_size))\n            xqkv_fused_sharded = xqkv_fused_sharded + self.wqkv_bias_decode[num_tiles - 1]\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(x)\n        xqkv_fused = tt_all_reduce(\n            xqkv_fused_sharded,\n            self.mesh_device,\n            self.tt_ccl,\n            cluster_axis=1,\n            num_reduce_scatter_links=self.num_reduce_scatter_links,\n            num_all_gather_links=self.num_all_gather_links,\n            memory_config=self.model_config[\"QKV_OUT_GATHERED_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[1]),\n            sharded=True,\n            dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n            topology=self.ccl_topology,\n        )\n\n        if self.TG:\n            # TODO: Slice the fused_query_key_value tensor get batch=8\n            xqkv_fused = ttnn.matmul(\n                self.slice_mat,\n                xqkv_fused,\n                dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n                memory_config=self.model_config[\"CREATE_HEAD_INPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n            )\n        else:\n            # bfloat16 is required by nlp_create_qkv_heads_decode\n            xqkv_fused = ttnn.sharded_to_interleaved(xqkv_fused_sharded, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG, ttnn.bfloat16)\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(xqkv_fused_sharded)\n\n        # Reshape such that true unpadded batch is tracked in shape\n        fqkv_shape = xqkv_fused.shape\n        xqkv_fused = ttnn.reshape(\n            xqkv_fused, (1, 1, self.batch_size_per_device_group, fqkv_shape[3]), (1, 1, 32, fqkv_shape[3])\n        )\n\n        ###\n        # Reshape and rotary embeddings\n        ###\n        (\n            q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD,\n            k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD,\n            v_heads_1BKD,\n        ) = ttnn.experimental.nlp_create_qkv_heads_decode(\n            xqkv_fused,\n            num_heads=self.n_local_heads,\n            num_kv_heads=self.n_local_kv_heads,\n            memory_config=self.model_config[\"CREATE_QKV_DECODE_SHARD\"],\n        )\n\n        q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD = self.q_norm(q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD, mode=\"decode\")\n        k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD = self.k_norm(k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD, mode=\"decode\")\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(xqkv_fused)\n\n        # Q Rotary Embeddings\n        q_heads_1BQD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n            q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD, rot_mats[0], rot_mats[1], self.transformation_mats[\"decode\"], is_decode_mode=True\n        )\n\n        # K Rotary Embeddings\n        k_heads_1BKD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n            k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD, rot_mats[0], rot_mats[1], self.transformation_mats[\"decode\"], is_decode_mode=True\n        )\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_pre_rot_1BQD)\n        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_pre_rot_1BKD)\n\n        ###\n        # KV update\n        ###\n        if kv_cache:\n            keys = kv_cache[0]\n            values = kv_cache[1]\n        else:\n            keys = self.layer_past[0]\n            values = self.layer_past[1]\n        # k_heads, [seqlen, n_kv_heads, bsz, head_dim]\n        # v_heads [seqlen, n_kv_heads, bsz, head_dim]\n        # keys, [max_batch_size, n_kv_heads // configuration.num_devices, max_seq_len, head_dim]\n        ttnn.experimental.paged_update_cache(keys, k_heads_1BKD, update_idxs_tensor=current_pos, page_table=page_table)\n        ttnn.experimental.paged_update_cache(\n            values, v_heads_1BKD, update_idxs_tensor=current_pos, page_table=page_table\n        )\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_1BKD)\n        ttnn.deallocate(v_heads_1BKD)\n\n        # NOTE: Varying the batch size will result in slightly different outputs.\n        # For example, a prompt w/ 1 user vs, the same prompt repeated N times for N users, will produce different outputs\n        # This is because the SDPA op in decode mode has different number of reductions depending on batch size\n        # Which leads to slightly different outputs from attention (due to accumulated errors)\n        if page_table:\n            attn_output_1G4D = ttnn.transformer.paged_scaled_dot_product_attention_decode(\n                q_heads_1BQD,\n                keys,\n                values,\n                cur_pos_tensor=current_pos,\n                page_table_tensor=page_table,\n                scale=self.scale,\n                sliding_window_size=self.sliding_window,\n                program_config=self.model_config[\"SDPA_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n            )\n        else:\n            attn_output_1G4D = ttnn.transformer.scaled_dot_product_attention_decode(\n                q_heads_1BQD,\n                keys,\n                values,\n                cur_pos_tensor=current_pos,\n                scale=self.scale,\n                sliding_window_size=self.sliding_window,\n                program_config=self.model_config[\"SDPA_DECODE_PROGCFG\"],\n                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n                memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,  # FIXME: why not L1 height sharded e.g. SCORES_BATCHED_MM_OUTPUT_MEMCFG?\n            )\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_1BQD)\n\n        attn_output_11BH = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n            attn_output_1G4D,\n            memory_config=self.model_config[\"SCORES_BATCHED_MM_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"](self.batch_size_per_device_group),\n        )\n        attn_output_cat = ttnn.experimental.nlp_concat_heads_decode(\n            attn_output_11BH,\n            num_heads=self.n_local_heads,\n        )\n        ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_11BH)\n        ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_1G4D)\n\n        if self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul:\n            attn_output_cat = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n                attn_output_cat, self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"]\n            )\n\n            # Fused AGMM only valid for ring topology\n            if self.ccl_topology == ttnn.Topology.Ring:\n                _, dense_out_sharded = ttnn.experimental.all_gather_matmul_async(\n                    attn_output_cat,\n                    self.wo,\n                    persistent_output_buffer=None,\n                    dim=3,\n                    multi_device_global_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_ag_semaphore_handles(),\n                    all_gather_core_grid_offset=(0, 4),\n                    barrier_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_barrier_semaphore_handle(),\n                    num_links=1,\n                    memory_config_ag=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n                    memory_config_mm=self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"],\n                    program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_PROGCFG\"],\n                    compute_kernel_config=self.compute_kernel_config_hifi2,\n                    chunks_per_sync=10,\n                    num_workers_per_link=2,\n                    num_buffers_per_channel=2,\n                )\n            else:\n                all_gather_output = ttnn.experimental.all_gather_async(\n                    attn_output_cat,\n                    persistent_output_buffer=None,\n                    dim=3,\n                    multi_device_global_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_ag_semaphore_handles(),\n                    num_links=1,\n                    topology=self.ccl_topology,\n                    memory_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_OUTPUT_MEMCFG\"],\n                    barrier_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_barrier_semaphore_handle(),\n                    chunks_per_sync=10,\n                    num_workers_per_link=2,\n                    num_buffers_per_channel=2,\n                )\n\n                dense_out_sharded = ttnn.linear(\n                    all_gather_output,\n                    self.wo,\n                    memory_config=self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"],\n                    program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_ALL_GATHER_MATMUL_PROGCFG\"],\n                    compute_kernel_config=self.li_o_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n                )\n\n                ttnn.deallocate(all_gather_output)\n            ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_cat)\n            dense_out_sharded = ttnn.to_memory_config(dense_out_sharded, self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"])\n            return dense_out_sharded\n\n        else:\n            attn_output = tt_all_gather(\n                attn_output_cat,\n                self.mesh_device,\n                self.tt_ccl,\n                dim=2,\n                cluster_axis=1,\n                num_links=2,\n                memory_config=self.model_config[\"GATHER_USERS_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[1]),\n                sharded=True,\n                # dtype=self.ccl_dtype,  # Running bf16 until we have SDPA output bfp8 df; otherwise we have two sharded to interleaved/interleaved to sharded conversions\n            )\n            if self.TG:\n                attn_output = ttnn.to_memory_config(attn_output, ttnn.L1_MEMORY_CONFIG)\n                # user_selection_matrix = [1, 1, 32, 128]\n                # user_selection_matrix @ activation -> [1, 1, 32, 128] * [1, 1, 128, 2048] -> [1, 1, 32, 2048]\n                attn_output = ttnn.matmul(\n                    self.user_selection_matrix,\n                    attn_output,\n                    core_grid=ttnn.CoreGrid(y=4, x=8),\n                    dtype=ttnn.bfloat16,\n                    memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n                )\n\n            # TODO: Fix this once self.TG supports dram-sharded matmuls\n            dense_out_sharded = ttnn.matmul(\n                attn_output,\n                self.wo,\n                core_grid=ttnn.CoreGrid(y=4, x=8) if self.TG else None,\n                program_config=self.model_config[\"ATTN_OUTPUT_PROGCFG\"] if not self.TG else None,\n                memory_config=ttnn.L1_WIDTH_SHARDED_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n                dtype=ttnn.bfloat8_b if self.TG else None,\n                compute_kernel_config=self.li_o_decode_compute_kernel_cfg,\n            )\n\n            ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_cat)\n\n            # All reduce\n            dense_out_reduced = tt_all_reduce(\n                dense_out_sharded,\n                self.mesh_device,\n                self.tt_ccl,\n                cluster_axis=0,\n                num_reduce_scatter_links=self.num_reduce_scatter_links,\n                num_all_gather_links=self.num_all_gather_links,\n                dim=0 if (self.TG and self.hidden_size < 8192) else 3,\n                topology=self.ccl_topology,\n                memory_config=(\n                    (\n                        self.model_config[\"SELF_OUT_REDUCE_SCATTER_MEMCFG\"]\n                        if self.hidden_size == 8192\n                        else self.model_config[\"SELF_OUT_GATHERED_MEMCFG\"](list(self.mesh_device.shape)[0])\n                    )\n                    if self.TG\n                    else self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"]\n                ),\n                sharded=True,\n                dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n                use_composite=True if self.hidden_size == 8192 else False,\n            )\n\n            if not self.TG:\n                dense_out_reduced = ttnn.to_memory_config(\n                    dense_out_reduced, self.model_config[\"DECODE_RESIDUAL_MEMCFG\"]\n                )\n\n            return dense_out_reduced\n\n    def forward_prefill(\n        self,\n        x_11SH,\n        rot_mats,\n        user_id: int = 0,\n        page_table=None,\n        chunk_page_table=None,\n        chunk_start_idx=None,\n        kv_cache=None,\n    ):\n        seq_len = x_11SH.shape[-2]\n        assert seq_len % 128 == 0 and seq_len > 0, \"Seqlen must be divisible by 128\"\n        ###\n        # QKV matmuls\n        ###\n\n        # reshaping long sequence to matmul fit on device\n        if seq_len > self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN:\n            if seq_len % self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN != 0:\n                raise ValueError(f\"seq_len {seq_len} must be divisible by {self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN}\")\n            x_11SH = ttnn.reshape(x_11SH, [1, seq_len // self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN, self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN, -1])\n\n        xqkv_fused = ttnn.linear(\n            x_11SH,\n            self.wqkv,\n            dtype=self.ccl_dtype if self.TG else self.activation_dtype or ttnn.bfloat16,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n            compute_kernel_config=self.li_qkv_prefill_compute_kernel_cfg,\n            program_config=self.model_config[\"XQKV_PREFILL_PROGCFG\"](seq_len),\n        )\n\n        # FIXME: surely ttnn.linear bias should work?\n        if self.wqkv_bias_prefill is not None:\n            xqkv_fused = xqkv_fused + self.wqkv_bias_prefill\n\n        xqkv_fused = tt_all_reduce(\n            xqkv_fused,\n            self.mesh_device,\n            self.tt_ccl,\n            cluster_axis=1,\n            num_reduce_scatter_links=self.num_reduce_scatter_links,\n            num_all_gather_links=self.num_all_gather_links,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n            dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n        )\n\n        if seq_len > self.MAX_QKV_MM_SEQ_LEN:\n            xqkv_fused = ttnn.reshape(xqkv_fused, [1, 1, seq_len, -1])\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(x_11SH)\n\n        # split qkv into heads\n        (\n            q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot,\n            k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot,\n            v_heads_1VSD,\n        ) = ttnn.experimental.nlp_create_qkv_heads(\n            xqkv_fused,\n            num_heads=self.n_local_heads,\n            num_kv_heads=self.n_local_kv_heads,\n            transpose_k_heads=False,\n            memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n        )\n\n        q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot = self.q_norm(q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot, mode=\"prefill\")\n        k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot = self.k_norm(k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot, mode=\"prefill\")\n\n        ttnn.deallocate(xqkv_fused)\n\n        ###\n        # Rotary embeddings\n        ###\n\n        if q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot.dtype != ttnn.bfloat16:  # Rotary embeddings require bfloat16 inputs\n            q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot = ttnn.typecast(q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot, dtype=ttnn.bfloat16)\n\n        q_heads_1QSD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n            q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot,\n            rot_mats[0],\n            rot_mats[1],\n            self.transformation_mats[\"prefill\"],\n            is_decode_mode=False,\n        )\n        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_1QSD_pre_rot)\n\n        if k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot.dtype != ttnn.bfloat16:  # Rotary embeddings require bfloat16 inputs\n            k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot = ttnn.typecast(k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot, dtype=ttnn.bfloat16)\n\n        k_heads_1KSD = ttnn.experimental.rotary_embedding_llama(\n            k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot,\n            rot_mats[0],\n            rot_mats[1],\n            self.transformation_mats[\"prefill\"],\n            is_decode_mode=False,\n        )\n        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_1KSD_pre_rot)\n\n        # Fill KV-Cache\n        if kv_cache:\n            keys_BKSD, values_BKSD = kv_cache[0], kv_cache[1]\n        else:\n            keys_BKSD, values_BKSD = self.layer_past[0], self.layer_past[1]\n        k_heads_1KSD_8b = ttnn.typecast(k_heads_1KSD, dtype=keys_BKSD.dtype)\n        ttnn.deallocate(k_heads_1KSD)\n\n        # sharding k_fill to deal with update_cache memory limitation\n        if seq_len >= self.min_kv_prefill_shard_seqlen and not self.TG and not page_table:\n            k_fill = ttnn.interleaved_to_sharded(k_heads_1KSD_8b, self.model_config[\"KV_PREFILL_MEM_CFG\"](seq_len))\n        else:\n            k_fill = k_heads_1KSD_8b\n\n        v_heads_1VSD_8b = with the unpadded k/v values.\n            # Assume that the page table does not have padding, so we can use it to get the unpadded page len.\n            block_size = keys_BKSD.shape[2]\n            # If chunked prefill, use chunk_page_table if given, otherwise use page_table.\n            fill_page_table = chunk_page_table if chunk_page_table is not None else page_table\n\n            page_len = ttnn.experimental.paged_fill_cache(values_BKSD, v_fill_sliced, fill_page_table, batch_idx=user_id)\n        else:\n            ttnn.fill_cache(\n                keys_BKSD,\n                k_fill,\n self.batch_size_per_device_group,\n            )\n        if seq_len >= self.min_kv_prefill_shard_seqlen and not self.TG and not page_table:\n            ttnn.deallocate(k_fill)\n            ttnn.deallocate(v_fill)\n\n        # SDPA\n        q_heads_1QSD_8b = ttnn.typecast(q_heads_1QSD, dtype=self.activation_dtype or ttnn.bfloat8_b)\n        ttnn.deallocate(q_heads_1QSD)\n\n        if input_tensor_v=values_BKSD,\n                page_table_tensor=page_table,\n                chunk_start_idx=chunk_start_idx,\n                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_prefill_compute_kernel_cfg,\n                program_config=self.model_config[\"SDPA_PROGCFG\"](seq_len),\n            )\n        else:\n            attn_output_84SD = sliding_window_size=self.sliding_window,\n                scale=self.scale,\n                compute_kernel_config=self.sdpa_prefill_compute_kernel_cfg,\n ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_1QSD)\n        # reshaping long sequence to matmul fit on device\n        if seq_len > 1024:\n            attn_output_11SH = ttnn.reshape(attn_output_11SH, [1, seq_len // memory_config=ttnn.DRAM_MEMORY_CONFIG,\n                barrier_semaphore=self.tt_ccl.get_and_cycle_barrier_semaphore_handle(),\n                chunks_per_sync=10,\n                num_workers_per_link=2,\n                num_buffers_per_channel=2,\n            )\n\n        output_11SH = ttnn.linear(\n            attn_output_11SH,\n            self.wo,\n program_config=self.model_config[\"WO_PREFILL_PROGCFG\"](seq_len),\n        )\n\n        if seq_len > 1024:\n            output_11SH = ttnn.reshape(output_11SH, [1, 1, seq_len, -1])\n        ttnn.deallocate(attn_output_11SH)\n\n        # Reduce-scatter\n        if not self.use_fused_all_gather_matmul:\n            output_11SH = tt_all_reduce(\n                output_11SH,\n dtype=self.ccl_dtype,\n            )\n\n        return output_11SH\n\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        x,\n        current_pos,\n        rot_mats=None,\n        user_id=0,\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HwuX7PQMRqb53NZakm7xay","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: © 2024 Tenstorrent AI ULC\n     2→\n     3→# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n     4→\n     5→import math\n     6→import os\n     7→import pathlib\n     8→from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union\n     9→\n    10→import ttnn.decorators\n    11→from loguru import logger\n    12→\n    13→import ttnn\n    14→\n    15→\n    16→def _validate_file_extension(file_name: pathlib.Path):\n    17→    if not str(file_name).endswith(\".tensorbin\"):\n    18→        raise RuntimeError(f\"File {file_name} must have .tensorbin extension\")\n    19→\n    20→\n    21→def _golden_function(input_tensor: ttnn.Tensor, slices):\n    22→    output_tensor = input_tensor[slices]\n    23→    if output_tensor.ndim == 0:\n    24→        raise RuntimeError(\"ttnn.Tensor.__getitem__: cannot return a scalar!\")\n    25→    return output_tensor\n    26→\n    27→\n    28→def _host_slice_with_unpad(input_tensor: ttnn.Tensor, begins, ends) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n    29→    \"\"\"Hacky fallback to old `unpad` methods for host based accessing\"\"\"\n    30→\n    31→    working_tensor = ttnn.to_layout(input_tensor, ttnn.ROW_MAJOR_LAYOUT).unpad(begins, ends)\n    32→    working_tensor = ttnn.view(working_tensor, [e - b for e, b in zip(ends, begins)])\n    33→    return ttnn.to_layout(working_tensor, input_tensor.get_layout())\n    34→\n    35→\n    36→@ttnn.register_python_operation(\n    37→    name=\"ttnn.Tensor.__getitem__\",\n    38→    is_method=True,\n    39→    golden_function=_golden_function,\n    40→)\n    41→def __getitem__(input_tensor: ttnn.Tensor, slices) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n    42→    \"\"\"\n    43→    Mimics PyTorch-style indexing for ttnn.Tensor using ttnn.slice and ttnn.squeeze.\n    44→    \"\"\"\n    45→\n    46→    # Gather some basic info\n    47→    input_rank = len(input_tensor.shape)\n    48→    shape = input_tensor.shape  # Or input_tensor.logical_shape, depending on your library's conventions\n    49→\n    50→    # 1) Normalize slices into a tuple.\n    51→    #    e.g. if user wrote a[3], slices is just int(3), so wrap it into (3,).\n    52→    #    or if user wrote a[2:5], slices is slice(2,5).\n    53→    if isinstance(slices, (int, slice, type(...))):\n    54→        slices = (slices,)\n    55→    else:\n    56→        # ensure it's a tuple in case user wrote something like a[1, 2:5, ...]\n    57→        slices = tuple(slices)\n    58→\n    59→    # 2) Expand any bare Ellipsis into enough slice(None) to fill out to input_rank.\n    60→    #    But we have to do this carefully in the presence of other slices.\n    61→    #    We'll do it in two passes:\n    62→    #      - first copy slices to a “normalized_slices”, remembering where Ellipsis is\n    63→    #      - then replace the Ellipsis with however many slice(None) are needed\n    64→    normalized_slices = []\n    65→    ellipsis_found = False\n    66→\n    67→    for s in slices:\n    68→        if s is Ellipsis:\n    69→            if ellipsis_found:\n    70→                raise ValueError(\"Only one ellipsis ('...') is allowed in a slice.\")\n    71→            # We'll deal with actually expanding it after this loop.\n    72→            ellipsis_found = True\n    73→            normalized_slices.append(Ellipsis)\n    74→        else:\n    75→            normalized_slices.append(s)\n    76→\n    77→    # If there's exactly one Ellipsis, expand it\n    78→    if ellipsis_found:\n    79→        ellipsis_index = normalized_slices.index(Ellipsis)\n    80→        # Number of slices ignoring the Ellipsis\n    81→        num_slices_no_ellipsis = len(normalized_slices) - 1\n    82→        # How many dimensions are “missing”\n    83→        num_missing = input_rank - num_slices_no_ellipsis\n    84→        if num_missing < 0:\n    85→            raise IndexError(f\"Too many indices for tensor of dimension {input_rank}\")\n    86→\n    87→        # Remove the Ellipsis placeholder\n    88→        del normalized_slices[ellipsis_index]\n    89→        # Insert slice(None) for however many dims are missing\n    90→        for _ in range(num_missing):\n    91→            normalized_slices.insert(ellipsis_index, slice(None, None, None))\n    92→\n    93→    # If there was no Ellipsis and we still have fewer slices than rank, pad with slice(None)\n    94→    while len(normalized_slices) < input_rank:\n    95→        normalized_slices.append(slice(None, None, None))\n    96→\n    97→    # Now if we have more slices than the rank, that’s an error\n    98→    if len(normalized_slices) > input_rank:\n    99→        raise IndexError(f\"Too many indices for tensor of dimension {input_rank}\")\n   100→\n   101→    # 3) Convert everything into slice objects (including integer indices),\n   102→    #    and record which dimensions we’ll need to squeeze out (integer-indexed dims).\n   103→    final_slices = []\n   104→    singled_out_dims = []  # dims where user gave an integer index\n   105→\n   106→    for dim_idx, s in enumerate(normalized_slices):\n   107→        if isinstance(s, int):\n   108→            # Negative index => convert as in python: s + size if s < 0\n   109→            idx = s if s >= 0 else (s + shape[dim_idx])\n   110→            if not 0 <= idx < shape[dim_idx]:\n   111→                raise IndexError(\n   112→                    f\"Index {s} (converted to {idx}) is out of bounds \"\n   113→                    f\"for dimension {dim_idx} of size {shape[dim_idx]}\"\n   114→                )\n   115→            final_slices.append(slice(idx, idx + 1, 1))\n   116→            singled_out_dims.append(dim_idx)\n   117→        elif isinstance(s, slice):\n   118→            # We mimic Python negative slicing for start/stop\n   119→            start, stop, step = s.start, s.stop, s.step\n   120→\n   121→            # default values\n   122→            if start is None:\n   123→                start = 0\n   124→            if stop is None:\n   125→                stop = shape[dim_idx]\n   126→            if step is None:\n   127→                step = 1\n   128→\n   129→            final_slices.append(slice(start, stop, step))\n   130→        else:\n   131→            raise TypeError(f\"Invalid slice type: {s}\")\n   132→\n   133→    # 4) Prepare the lists for ttnn.slice\n   134→    slice_start = []\n   135→    slice_end = []\n   136→    slice_step = []\n   137→\n   138→    for dim_idx, sl in enumerate(final_slices):\n   139→        # No further negative indexing needed: we already converted above\n   140→        slice_start.append(sl.start)\n   141→        slice_end.append(sl.stop)\n   142→        slice_step.append(sl.step)\n   143→\n   144→    # 5) Perform the slicing\n   145→    if ttnn.is_tensor_storage_on_device(input_tensor):\n   146→        output = ttnn.slice(input_tensor, slice_start, slice_end, slice_step)\n   147→    else:\n   148→        if not all([s == 1 for s in slice_step]):\n   149→            raise RuntimeError(\"Host tensors cannot be accessed with non-unit stride\")\n   150→        output = _host_slice_with_unpad(input_tensor, slice_start, slice_end)\n   151→\n   152→    # Gather some basic info\n    input_rank = len(input_tensor.shape)\n    shape = input_tensor.shape  # Or input_tensor.logical_shape, depending on your library's conventions\n\n    # 1) Normalize slices into a tuple.\n    #    e.g. if user wrote a[3], slices is just int(3), so wrap it into (3,).\n    #    or if user wrote a[2:5], slices is slice(2,5).\n    if isinstance(slices, (int, slice, type(...))):\n        slices = (slices,)\n    else:\n        # ensure it's a tuple in case user wrote something like a[1, 2:5, ...]\n        slices = tuple(slices)\n\n    # 2) Expand any bare Ellipsis into enough slice(None) to fill out to input_rank.\n    #    But we have to do this carefully in the presence of other slices.\n    #    We'll do it in two passes:\n    #      - first copy slices to a “normalized_slices”, remembering where Ellipsis is\n    #      - then replace the Ellipsis with however many slice(None) are needed\n    normalized_slices = []\n    ellipsis_found = False\n\n    for s in slices:\n        if s is Ellipsis:\n            if ellipsis_found:\n                raise ValueError(\"Only one ellipsis ('...') is allowed in a slice.\")\n            # We'll deal with actually expanding it after this loop.\n            ellipsis_found = True\n            normalized_slices.append(Ellipsis)\n        else:\n            normalized_slices.append(s)\n\n    # If there's exactly one Ellipsis, expand it\n    if ellipsis_found:\n        ellipsis_index = normalized_slices.index(Ellipsis)\n        # Number of slices ignoring the Ellipsis\n        num_slices_no_ellipsis = len(normalized_slices) - 1\n        # How many dimensions are “missing”\n        num_missing = input_rank - num_slices_no_ellipsis\n        if num_missing < 0:\n            raise IndexError(f\"Too many indices for tensor of dimension {input_rank}\")\n\n        # Remove the Ellipsis placeholder\n        del normalized_slices[ellipsis_index]\n        # Insert slice(None) for however many dims are missing\n        for _ in range(num_missing):\n            normalized_slices.insert(ellipsis_index, slice(None, None, None))\n\n    # If there was no Ellipsis and we still have fewer slices than rank, pad with slice(None)\n    while len(normalized_slices) < input_rank:\n        normalized_slices.append(slice(None, None, None))\n\n    # Now if we have more slices than the rank, that’s an error\n    if len(normalized_slices) > input_rank:\n        raise IndexError(f\"Too many indices for tensor of dimension {input_rank}\")\n\n    # 3) Convert everything into slice objects (including integer indices),\n    #    and record which dimensions we’ll need to squeeze out (integer-indexed dims).\n    final_slices = []\n    singled_out_dims = []  # dims where user gave an integer index\n\n    for dim_idx, s in enumerate(normalized_slices):\n        if isinstance(s, int):\n            # Negative index => convert as in python: s + size if s < 0\n            idx = s if s >= 0 else (s + shape[dim_idx])\n            if not 0 <= idx < shape[dim_idx]:\n                raise IndexError(\n                    f\"Index {s} (converted to {idx}) is out of bounds \"\n                    f\"for dimension {dim_idx} of size {shape[dim_idx]}\"\n                )\n            final_slices.append(slice(idx, idx + 1, 1))\n            singled_out_dims.append(dim_idx)\n        elif isinstance(s, slice):\n            # We mimic Python negative slicing for start/stop\n            start, stop, step = s.start, s.stop, s.step\n\n            # default values\n            if start is None:\n                start = 0\n            if stop is None:\n                stop = shape[dim_idx]\n            if step is None:\n                step = 1\n\n            final_slices.append(slice(start, stop, step))\n        else:\n            raise TypeError(f\"Invalid slice type: {s}\")\n\n    # 4) Prepare the lists for ttnn.slice\n    slice_start = []\n    slice_end = []\n    slice_step = []\n\n    for dim_idx, sl in enumerate(final_slices):\n        # No further negative indexing needed: we already converted above\n        slice_start.append(sl.start)\n        slice_end.append(sl.stop)\n        slice_step.append(sl.step)\n\n    # 5) Perform the slicing\n    if ttnn.is_tensor_storage_on_device(input_tensor):\n        output = ttnn.slice(input_tensor, slice_start, slice_end, slice_step)\n    else:\n        if not all([s == 1 for s in slice_step]):\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Host tensors cannot be accessed with non-unit stride\")\n        output = _host_slice_with_unpad(input_tensor, slice_start, slice_end)\n\n    # 6) Squeeze out all dimensions that were indexed by an integer.\n    #    We do this from left to right, adjusting each subsequent dimension index\n    #    or do it from right to left so we don't need to adjust. Either approach works\n    #    if we’re careful. The simplest is ascending order with a small counter.\n    shift = 0\n    for original_dim in sorted(singled_out_dims):\n        # after removing 'shift' dims, the current dim is (original_dim - shift)\n        squeeze_dim = original_dim - shift\n        # Squeeze only if that dim is actually size 1 after slicing\n        output = ttnn.squeeze(output, squeeze_dim)\n        shift += 1\n\n    return output\n\n\ndef _preprocess_shape(input_shape, shape):\n    if isinstance(shape, tuple):\n        if not (0 <= shape.count(-1) <= 1):\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Shape cannot have more than 1 elements that is set to -1!\")\n\n        volume = math.prod(input_shape)\n        new_volume = math.prod(shape)\n        if new_volume < 0:\n            index_of_negative_1 = shape.index(-1)\n            shape = list(shape)\n            shape[index_of_negative_1] = volume // (-new_volume)\n            shape = tuple(shape)\n        shape = ttnn.Shape(shape)\n    return shape\n\n\ndef _preprocess_golden_function_inputs(args, kwargs):\n    input_tensor, args, kwargs = ttnn.reflection.pop_argument(\"input_tensor\", args, kwargs)\n    shape, args, kwargs = ttnn.reflection.pop_argument(\"shape\", args, kwargs)\n    shape = _preprocess_shape(input_tensor.shape, shape)\n    input_tensor = input_tensor.reshape(input_tensor.padded_shape)\n    return (ttnn.to_torch(input_tensor), tuple(shape), *args), kwargs\n\n\ndef _golden_function(input_tensor, shape: Union[ttnn.Shape, Tuple[int, ...]]) -> ttnn.Tensor:\n    return input_tensor.reshape(shape).contiguous().clone()\n\n\ndef _postprocess_golden_function_outputs(output, args, kwargs):\n    tensor = ttnn.decorators.default_postprocess_golden_function_outputs(output, args, kwargs)\n\n    input_tensor, args, kwargs = ttnn.reflection.pop_argument(\"input_tensor\", args, kwargs)\n    shape, args, kwargs = ttnn.reflection.pop_argument(\"shape\", args, kwargs)\n    shape = _preprocess_shape(input_tensor.shape, shape)","numLines":200,"startLine":1,"totalLines":630}}}
